SurrealDB develops a multi-model database platform designed to handle document, graph, and relational data within a single system. The platform supports SQL-like querying while enabling real-time data synchronization and event-driven updates across applications. It provides built-in features such as authentication, authorization, and access control at the database layer. SurrealDB operates across various environments, including embedded deployments, single-node instances, and distributed clusters. The system is designed to support modern application development by combining flexible data modeling with transactional consistency and programmable logic inside the database.

Unleash provides a software platform used to manage feature flags within applications. The system allows development teams to enable or disable specific features without modifying or redeploying application code. It includes a management interface and API services that store feature flag configurations and distribute them to applications through software development kits. The platform supports configuration of activation strategies that determine when a feature becomes available based on defined rules, such as user segments, environments, or rollout percentages. It also allows the creation of feature variants that enable different versions of a feature to be shown to separate user groups for experimentation or testing.

Skye Air Mobility is a drone delivery startup that develops drone-based logistics systems that enable autonomous aerial delivery of packages for sectors such as e-commerce, healthcare, and retail. The company operates a technology platform that coordinates electric delivery drones, digital airspace management tools, and logistics software to transport parcels between distribution points and delivery locations. The company’s delivery system integrates aerial drones with supporting technologies such as autonomous ground robots, smart delivery lockers, and digital tracking platforms. Packages are transported by drone to a designated landing or collection point, where automated infrastructure or delivery personnel complete the final handover to customers. 0039 02 49517 001
Cleafy develops cybersecurity software that helps financial institutions detect and prevent online fraud across digital banking channels. The company provides a fraud management platform that monitors user activity across web, mobile, and API sessions to identify suspicious behavior before fraudulent transactions occur. Its technology analyzes interactions throughout a digital banking session, enabling banks to detect threats such as account takeover, phishing, and malware-based attacks early in the attack lifecycle. The platform combines cybersecurity and fraud detection capabilities in a single system that correlates behavioral signals, device information, and session activity to reconstruct attack patterns. By analyzing activity across multiple sessions and channels, the system helps financial institutions identify coordinated fraud campaigns and automated attacks targeting online banking services. Cleafy’s software provides real-time monitoring and response tools that allow security teams to intervene when suspicious activity is detected. Banks can trigger actions such as step-up authentication, blocking suspicious devices, or preventing transactions based on risk indicators generated by the platform. These capabilities help organizations reduce fraud losses while maintaining normal digital banking experiences for legitimate users.

Qurrent is an autonomous digital workforce company that provides digital workforces that execute complex back-office operations across functions such as finance, supply chain, procurement, and enterprise operations. The company deploys software-based digital workers that perform rule-based and cognitive tasks, automating workflows that traditionally require human intervention. These digital workforces are fully managed, with Qurrent owning the deployment, ongoing management, and performance of each digital worker while ensuring operational reliability and compliance with contractual service-level agreements (SLAs). The platform integrates with enterprise systems such as ERP, CRM, and procurement platforms to handle tasks including invoice processing, order management, reconciliations, and reporting. Its autonomous workers interact with multiple applications, validate data, and execute transactions across business systems, reducing manual effort and accelerating operational efficiency. Qurrent also monitors, maintains, and optimizes the performance of its digital workforce in real time. Performance metrics, process improvements, and compliance checks are embedded into its management framework, ensuring that automated operations meet agreed-upon business outcomes and quality standards. By providing a fully managed approach, the company allows organizations to implement robotic and cognitive automation without the need for in-house infrastructure, specialized staffing, or complex software management. Qurrent guarantees operational outcomes through contractual SLAs, making digital workforces a reliable extension of enterprise teams for back-office automation.

As a result SignalRank has 300+ seed partners who invite it to partner for pro-rata financing in their fastest growing companies. It sees a high percentage of all Series B term sheets prior to close. Capital is supplied from SignalRank’s balance sheet, with no capital calls or partner IC process delays. Check size correlates with capital available. SignalRank handles SPV setup and ongoing administration, with a target of moving from partner allocation request to funding in 5 business days. 20% of the profit on each investment goes directly to partners. SignalRank creates deal-by-deal SPVs; SignalRank does not charge management fees for this partner product. The portfolio can be seen at - https://signalrank.com/signalrank-index.html

They are leading in supply chain tech, and AI. The Journey Foods database retains an extensive and standardized database to extract data from millions of ingredients for algorithms to dynamically analyze and methodically recommended optimized portfolio recommendations based on pricing, supply chain, and nutrition parameters. To date, the company has established a database comprised of over 56 billion ingredient insights and learnings from our in-house prototyping product ‘Journey Bites’.

Revive was born to take on a supply chain challenge that every brand faces: What should we do with slightly imperfect returns, store try-ons, display samples, or just plain old excess inventory? Without actionable data, brands only have a few (not ideal) options: costly recycling - difficult when the materials are not built to be recycled easily; donations - also difficult since donation centers cannot process that much volume; and as the last resort, incineration or destruction of the goods. Current options offer no financial recovery, and brands expect to lose the majority of asset value for deadstock. Revive is a first-in-line solution. Before recycling or donating, let our AI-powered platform show the recovered value potential of this inventory. Let us help you make a decision of whether these small damages are worth cleaning and/or repairing to be sold. Based on your decision, Revive will help realize the maximum value of this neglected inventory.

zypl.ai
—

zypl.ai applies 'no data' AI at scale to advance financial inclusion in emerging and frontier markets. Our SaaS solution enables banks and microfinance organizations to instantly and accurate underwrite loans, both offline and digitally, to consumers with limited or no credit history. zypl's proprietary approach includes training AI models on synthetically generated data with simulation of 'black swan' conditions, providing stable and adaptable SaaS in spite of macro volatility in developing markets. Apart from analyzing traditional variables such as social, demographic, and professional background of clients, our SaaS reviews device metadata, psychometrics, web trace, and macroeconomic factors to arrive at credit decisions. In the process, we fully customize AI models to risk appetite of each individual enterprise partner with the goal of reflecting their competitive market advantages. Additionally, we offer complete flexibility on mode of integration through either cloud or on-premise deployment based on enterprise preference. We only deploy explainable AI models with embedded 'weight-of-evidence' interpretation of each decision made by the software, complying with emerging regulatory requirements on credit scoring. Having launched our beta last year, we have scaled presence to 10 enterprise clients in 4 markets across Central Asia. In this time, we have underwritten >$9 million in consumer and small business (services, trade, agriculture) loans to over 12 thousand consumers with staggeringly low 0.7% portfolio-at-risk rate. Since booking first revenue earlier this year, we have surpassed 25% compounded monthly growth rate and reached financial break-even this month. As a complement to credit scoring, our product team has ventured into churn prediction in telecom sector through partnerships with 3 leading telcos in Central Asia. Our rapid product iteration and development is a result of an AI Academy that we have launched to nurture talent internally. zypl.ai was ideated at Stanford University's Startup Garage and recently completed the Silkway Accelerator by Google for Startups and Astana Hub. Our startup was launched with a grant from the Islamic Development Bank as part of the Transform Fund. With our pre-seed capital raise, we plan on globalizing our AI SaaS solution by expanding to new markets in MENA, Southeast Asia, and Eastern Europe.

13,089
$1M to $10M
For Profit
trustifi.com
View on Twitter
View on LinkedIn
info@trustificorp.com
1-877-404-8525
Trustifi is an email visibility and security company providing a SaaS solution that protects businesses from email vulnerabilities and compliance risks. Basic email is extremely vulnerable to being breached because cybercriminals are always looking to steal private and Personally Identifiable Information (PII). As stolen data is worth a lot of money, it results in real harm to the victims when information is exposed, plus, big ¬fines for the companies who unintentionally expose it. Trustifi’s easy-to-use solution provides certified and Postmarked proof of email delivery with built-in email encryption and security for the ultimate in email visibility & protection. With Trustifi, sent emails are tracked and legally Postmarked with records of delivery and exact content delivered. Plus, visibility to when the email is opened, by whom, in real-time. Trustifi uses 256-bit military-grade encryption to secure outgoing emails and recipients are able to open encrypted emails with ease, without downloading or using special software to receive, open and reply encrypted. Trustifi also adds many layers of email security with such features as: Fraudulent Email Detection - Trustifi alerts users when incoming scam emails, designed to look legitimate for the purposes tricking employees into wiring money or clicking malware links, are of a fraudulent nature and, Content Filtering Protection - Trustifi scans the content of an email as it’s being written and if the email contains any sensitive words or phrases that should be encrypted, the sender is alerted to the fact that Trustifi’s automatic encryption has been triggered.

revaliabio.com

contactus@revaliabio.com

At Revalia we make it possible to study human disease directly in living organs, revealing insights into human biology that were previously impossible. By reviving donated human organs with advanced perfusion technology, we generate a new kind of integrated human data that captures the full complexity of human biology. These living organs act as the Rosetta Stone of Human Data because they allow us to align patient history, anatomy, physiology, cellular, and molecular profiles into a single complete picture. Through what we call Human Data Trials, we then transform this information into decision grade evidence on safety, dosing, and efficacy — all before putting a single patient at risk. Just as importantly, Human Data Trials create a new path for organ donation when a donor’s organs cannot be used for clinical transplant. This path transforms the loss of one life into discoveries with the potential to unlock the future of medicine and thereby create a lasting legacy for that patient and their family.

Verisoul provides an AI-driven identity verification and fraud-prevention platform designed to help online businesses distinguish real users from bots, fake accounts, and coordinated abuse. The company’s products analyze behavioral signals, device attributes, network patterns, and identity indicators to assess whether an account or transaction is operated by a genuine human. Verisoul offers tools for user onboarding protection, account integrity monitoring, and ongoing risk assessment across sign-ups, logins, and in-app activity. Its platform supports use cases such as preventing fake account creation, detecting multi-accounting, reducing promotional abuse, and identifying automated or scripted behavior. Verisoul delivers these capabilities through APIs and dashboards that integrate into customer workflows and provide actionable risk scores and alerts. The service is used by digital platforms seeking to maintain trust, reduce fraud-related losses, and protect community quality without relying solely on traditional CAPTCHA or document-based verification methods.

Finmile is the delivery execution operating system for the world outside Amazon. Every ecommerce brand is great at selling. Almost none are great at delivering. The moment an order is placed, execution leaves the building and is handed to fragmented operators running static routes, manual exception handling, and disconnected tools. Finmile replaces this chaos with a single, operator first delivery operating system. The platform runs last mile execution end to end, from order ingestion and label creation through live routing, dispatch, driver operations, customer communication, proof of delivery, returns, and billing. Unlike traditional routing or visibility tools, Finmile remains active once drivers are on the road, continuously reoptimising routes, resolving exceptions, and enforcing outcomes in real time. Finmile’s AI does not simply surface insights or suggest actions. It automates the work planners and customer support teams do today, with a human in the loop only when confidence is low. This allows operators to scale delivery volume without scaling headcount. Customers use Finmile to materially improve unit economics and service levels, achieving up to 42 percent fewer routes, 92 percent reduction in WISMO enquiries, and 82 percent fewer delivery claims, while improving first attempt success and reliability. Finmile is used by logistics operators, carriers, and brands across the United Kingdom, Europe, and the United States, running hundreds of thousands of deliveries per day. The business is profitable, with recurring and expanding revenue, and becomes core infrastructure once deployed. Amazon built this capability for itself. Shopify did it for commerce. Finmile is building it for delivery execution.

Piece is a platform where you can invest in already profitable properties, from just 100€. The platform is designed to make property investment accessible, transparent, and intuitive. The latest version elevates the experience with a refined interface that gives investors a clear overview of their portfolios, earnings, and ongoing opportunities. New property offerings are introduced regularly, allowing users to begin investing immediately after completing KYC. Each opportunity is structured as a tokenized security linked to a single property, featuring a defined maturity, quarterly interest payouts, and potential bonus interest at the end of the term. A built-in Secondary Market enhances liquidity by enabling the purchase and sale of Pieces once the initial offering closes. All products comply with the European MiFID II framework and operate under BaFin supervision, ensuring a high standard of transparency and investor protection. Piece continues to evolve through user feedback, aiming to set a new benchmark for accessible, data-driven real estate investing.

Chata.ai offers end-to-end data architecture services, preparing your data for AI, delivering advanced data alerts, and enabling data decentralization through natural language. Our solution includes data preparation, custom language model creation using proprietary technology (without relying on LLMs), deployment, end-user onboarding, and API support. We specialize in consolidating tables and integrating diverse data sources based on business priorities, seamlessly connecting with systems like ERP, SAP, and enterprise tools to eliminate “data islands” and provide unified insights. Chata.ai delivers proactive, real-time alerts on key metrics, helping businesses stay ahead of issues and reduce the need for constant monitoring. Users can then explore complex data through natural language queries, gaining insights without needing technical expertise. Our intuitive dashboards and visualizations help uncover deeper insights and empower teams to make data-driven decisions quickly. We focus on efficiency and scalability, offering seamless integration through open APIs and customization for enterprise environments. Our CPU-based inference reduces operational costs by up to 99.8% compared to traditional GPU-based models. Additionally, we ensure complete data security by deploying within the customer’s infrastructure, keeping all sensitive data in-house. Chata.ai serves companies to harmonize fragmented data, improve service efficiency, and drive informed decision-making at scale.

Octave Logo
Octave
High
Feb 2022
High

Active
May 23, 2025
Seed
Generative AI, Natural Language Processing, SaaS, Sales Enablement
San Francisco, California, United States
Octave provides an AI‑driven generative go‑to‑market platform that powers contextualized messaging and outbound sales workflows.
24,296
—
For Profit
www.octavehq.com/
—
View on LinkedIn
support@octavehq.com
—
GTM is changing at a relentless and possibly unprecedented pace. The end of zirp and rise of AI have combined to end the predictable revenue era. Most of the 'modern' systems, practices, and processes that companies rely on today were born and cultivated in this era. But interestingly, many believe the tools developed in this era are also pre-ordained to win the next one. We couldn't disagree more. GTM is moving from the era of automation, to the era of generation. From a world of complex, admin-centric tools, based on static data and rigid rules, to a dynamic world where end users are directly empowered with real-time data, multiplayer collaboration, elegant workflows, and the power of infinite flexibility and personalization with AI. To capture this potential, things must be rebuilt from the ground up; a simple retrofit with AI pixie dust won't cut it. We've embarked on an ambitious journey to build a new platform that doesn't exist, and couldn't exist until now. The fatal limiting flaw of all GTM tools is that they are either built on top of activity data, deal data, and/or contact data. While this data is no doubt important, there's a truly fundamental piece of the puzzle it can't tell you a single thing about… what the heck are you actually selling?! GTM tools don't have a clue about what your products actually are, who they're for, how they're used, or why they provide value. Keeping track of this core knowledge (i.e. your messaging and positioning) has always been left outside of a purpose built system - it's been incumbent upon people to remember what they need to know, and create from memory (over and over again). This knowledge is also not connected in any way to core selling workflows - the vast majority of 'how you actually sell' is tribal knowledge that must be manually turned into productive actions by each individual. Octave is a new workflow-oriented platform that's built on top of the data format we believe will power the next era of GTM - context. Imagine a tool that's architected to understand the context of your unique GTM - it knows your ICPs, your value props, your personas, your use cases, competitors, differentiators, etc.

This software is particularly beneficial for businesses looking to streamline their MDM processes and achieve compliance with frameworks such as SOC2, ISO, NIST, and GDPR in a fraction of the time typically required. With Swif.ai, companies can transition from zero to compliance readiness in just one hour, making it an efficient choice for organizations of all sizes. Targeted primarily at businesses with employee counts ranging from 5 to 50,000, Swif.ai caters to a diverse audience, including small startups, mid-sized companies, and large enterprises. The software addresses the unique challenges that organizations face in managing mobile devices, such as data security, device tracking, and regulatory compliance. By providing a comprehensive MDM solution, Swif.ai enables companies to focus on their core operations while ensuring that their mobile device management is handled effectively and securely. One of the standout features of Swif.ai is its inclusion of free Shadow IT management for all customers. This capability allows organizations to identify and manage unauthorized applications and devices that may pose security risks. By integrating Shadow IT management into the MDM framework, Swif.ai enhances overall security posture and helps organizations maintain compliance with regulatory requirements. This feature is particularly valuable in today’s environment, where the use of personal devices for work purposes is increasingly common. In addition to its robust compliance capabilities, Swif.ai offers a user-friendly interface that simplifies the management of mobile devices. The software provides tools for device enrollment, policy enforcement, and remote management, allowing IT teams to efficiently oversee their mobile fleets. The ability to quickly implement security policies and monitor device compliance ensures that organizations can respond promptly to potential threats and maintain a secure operating environment. Overall, Swif.ai distinguishes itself in the MDM software market by combining rapid compliance capabilities with comprehensive mobile device management features. Its focus on serving a wide range of organizations, from small businesses to large enterprises, along with its unique offerings like free Shadow IT management, positions it as a valuable solution for companies looking to enhance their mobile device security and compliance efforts.

Millions of talented students can't afford to pursue their degrees and careers in Europe, and at the same time, Europe is suffering from a major labour shortage. Lendorse provides a "study now, pay later" agreement specifically for students from developing countries. Lendorse has developed a proprietary underwriting model that selects creditworthiness without assessing credit scores, collateral or co-signers to increase access to finance and therefore education. Once the students graduate, Lendorse helps the students land their dream job, and they share a portion of their income back with Lendorse for a few years (called an "Income Share Agreement"). With a term sheet for €10M debt funds secured, Lendorse will sponsor >1000 students in the next 24 months, locking in a minimum of €1M revenue. The European Investment Fund is a guarantor for Lendorse's potential defaults, and will reimburse up to 104% per defaulted Income Share Agreement, up to a total of €2M in value.
